# PROVENANCE — 13-rainbor-light-stones-matrix-2026-08-10

One row per vendored file. Sources are public, read-only, all under `JesseBrown1980/`: `RAINBOR`, `LIGHT---lIFEds6-7`, `THE-STAR-PORTS`, `Glyphs-Actual-glyphs---gnns-and-tables-needed-to-rebuild-the-system`, `asolaria-quantum-universe-alive` and `asolaria-civilisation-matrix` (one branch each), `asolaria-stone-to-moss` (123 branches and every pull-request head) and `asolaria-federation-1024` (23 branches and every pull-request ref), each read from a bare `blob:none` clone of the repository's refs. Each file was read with `git cat-file -p <blob>`. `blob` is the git blob id; `git hash-object --no-filters` of the vendored file equals it (verified, 771/771), and `git rev-parse <commit>:<path>` in the source repository equals it (verified, 771/771). `sha256` is the first 16 hex of the full file sha256 (full digests in `UPSTREAM.sha256`; `sha256sum -c` 771/771 OK). `source @ commit : path` names the commit the bytes were read from: the branch head of each repository at the time of the inventory, except `asolaria-stone-to-moss/.gitignore`, which is read from `b798beb`, the last commit of the 2026-10-08 release. `.gitignore` is absent at the head `97174e7`: `4fd4fe1` (2026-10-08 21:33:32 -03:00) added the vendored blob `c18f64e8`; `c348fba` (2026-10-14 13:03:52 -03:00, "Exclude physical-seat private keys and secret salts from Git") replaced it with `52d8b923` (listed with the other versions below); `0e1456f` (2026-10-17 06:26:26 -03:00, "Remove root .gitignore as requested", an ancestor of `97174e7`) deleted it. The heads were remeasured with `git ls-remote` on 2026-10-19T12:01:06Z (last section). `beginning` names the commit that first introduced that exact blob, at any path on any ref of the source repository (`git log --all --find-object=<blob>`; committer date converted to -03:00; for all 771 rows the last line of that log is also the earliest commit). For a blob read from a later commit than the one that introduced it, the two differ but the bytes are identical. Three blobs are vendored at more than one path because each path is a separate upstream file (771 rows, 766 distinct blobs): the `.nojekyll.sha256` sidecar `e35e3d19` (75 B) in `RAINBOR`, `LIGHT---lIFEds6-7`, `THE-STAR-PORTS` and `Glyphs-Actual-glyphs---gnns-and-tables-needed-to-rebuild-the-system`; the seat-1 triad challenge `b098e2ce` (723 B) at `experiments/seat1/TRIAD-27-CHALLENGE-20261014.json` and `experiments/seat3/seat1-triad-challenge-pinned.json`; and `003817dc` (3,095 B) at `RESULTS.json` and `RESULTS-R1.json` of `stones/2026-10-15-asolaria-trit-recurrence/`.

`tracking/STATUS.md`, `tracking/latest.json` and the 26 `tracking/snapshots/` pairs are written by `github-actions[bot]` (`[tracker] Record three-seat public activity snapshot`: the workflow `.github/workflows/three-seat-activity.yml`, pinned above, runs the vendored `tools/collect_three_seat_activity.py`). They are GitHub commit, branch, pull-request and workflow metadata, not seat work, and the bot keeps adding to them: `STATUS.md` and `latest.json` are rewritten at every snapshot (26 blobs each on all refs up to `97174e7`), so their 25 earlier versions each are not listed with the other versions below; each snapshot file was written once. The 39 other files under `tracking/` (seat check-ins, peer and cross-seat audits, the ledger, the seat registry and the verified-status notes) were committed with the seats' work, not by the bot, and are vendored.

### Paths that never reached `main` (303 paths, 415 blobs, 4,064,569 B)

These paths exist only on agent branches and pull-request heads of `asolaria-stone-to-moss` that were not merged (`.gitignore`, removed from `main`, is the only other path missing at `97174e7` and is vendored). By first commit, 8 paths are from 10-14, 210 from 10-15 and 85 from 10-16. They include the Proxima and alpha-Cen rounds (`experiments/seat1/proxima-rust181/`, `experiments/seat4/proxima-*`, `experiments/claude-verify/alphacen-gguf-constellation-20261016/`), the seat-5 rounds, the physical-host rounds (`seat1_photon_phonon/`, `seat2/round6` to `round9`, `seat4/round6` to `round14`), the neural light bank and the photon-electron cross-examination. Every blob that ever stood at one of these paths is a row. `ref` names one branch that contains the commit (with the number of other branches that also do); the pull-request heads are listed where no branch does. `experiments/seat2/round9/review_host_bridge.js` at `ceb57bb8`, the "original frozen Seat2" reviewer that the vendored `experiments/seat3/pr111_custody/` preregistration and results name, is the version at PR #111's frozen head `8e597ae`.

### Inputs named by vendored files outside these heads

A scan of every vendored text file of `asolaria-stone-to-moss` for repository paths (full, relative and unique base names) and for blob ids finds no file of `asolaria-stone-to-moss` that is not vendored, in aether or pinned above. The earlier version of this section pinned four such inputs; they are now vendored with the rest of `main` (`experiments/seat1/TRIAD-27-CHALLENGE-20261014.json`, `experiments/seat3/verify_public_seat3.js`, `experiments/seat3/two_horizon_matrix.py`, `tools/audit_four_seat_release.py`). Two kinds of inputs lie in other repositories.

**The N-Nest upstream of the seat-4 autonomous capsule.** `experiments/seat4/package_autonomous_capsule.py` packs its 23 `SEAT4_SOURCES`, the six files of the seat-1 triad receipt and two capsule files (all 31 vendored here) with every file of `JesseBrown1980/N-Nest-Prime-INFINITE-SELF-REFLECT-AGENTS-NESTED` at `N_NEST = 307903651f56e8a28ef98ca36aef22ee8cda935c` (`main`, 2026-08-08 18:09:21 -03:00, "star port sidecar"), and asserts that there are 23. 7 of the 23 are already in aether or vendored here under another path; the other 16 are pinned here. The packager reads all 23 from a checkout of that repository at that commit (`--nnest`), so running it still needs the public repository; with these pins every file it packs is identified by blob id and sha256.

| path | blob | bytes | sha256 | beginning (N-Nest) | where |

## Upstream .sha256 sidecars

207 of the vendored files are upstream `.sha256` files. 206 are sidecars: each names the sha256 of its companion file, and all 206 agree with the measured bytes of that companion. 184 companions are vendored here, 16 are pinned blobs (the minute-stone bodies), and 6 are already in aether (`Glyphs-Actual-glyphs---gnns-and-tables-needed-to-rebuild-the-system : .nojekyll`, `LIGHT---lIFEds6-7 : .nojekyll`, `RAINBOR : .nojekyll`, `THE-STAR-PORTS : .nojekyll`, `asolaria-civilisation-matrix : RIME-SPHERES.hbi`, `asolaria-stone-to-moss : INDEX.hbi`).
